002   Shenzhen Investment Limited 深圳控股有限公司
      Annual Report 年報 2024




 Corporate Profile
 公司簡介



                                   The Company intensifies its development in
                                   Shenzhen and focuses on the Guangdong-
                                   Hong Kong-Macao Greater Bay Area


                                                Shenzhen Investment Limited has been listed on the Main Board of The
                                                Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited since 1997. Shum Yip Group, being its
                                                controlling shareholder, is an integrated conglomerate directly managed by
                                                State-owned Assets Supervision and Administration Commission of Shenzhen
                                                Municipal. Positioned as an “Innovative Constructor of Industrial Cities,



 公司深耕深圳
                                                Wealthy Livelihood Operator”, the Company’s core businesses include property
                                                development, property investment and comprehensive urban operation services,



 聚焦粤港澳
                                                and it also engages in innovative businesses such as high-tech agriculture
                                                and high-end manufacturing, endeavoring to transform and upgrade into a



  大灣區
                                                technology-based industry group focusing on the development of urban complex
                                                and investment in technology industry.

                                                By intensifying its development in Shenzhen, focusing on the Guangdong-Hong
                                                Kong-Macao Greater Bay Area and planning for its development in the Yangtze
                                                River Delta and high-tier cities such as key provincial capital cities, the Group
                                                currently has a land reserve with gross floor area of approximately 6.22 million
                                                square meters in various cities of China, of which the quality land reserve with
                                                gross floor area of approximately 4.13 million square meters is located in the
                                                Guangdong-Hong Kong-Macao Greater Bay Area. The Company is committed to
                                                optimizing its land reserves structure, and concentrates its assets and business
                                                in the Guangdong-Hong Kong-Macao Greater Bay Area as well as high-tier cities
                                                in key regions.

                                                It has all along been the Company’s core strategic goal to promote the long term
                                                sustainable development of its business, with an aim to bring a stable return and
                                                create value appreciation for its shareholders.

                                                深圳控股有限公司於1997年在香港聯合交易所有限公司主板上市，其控股股東
                                                深業集團是深圳市國資委直管的大型綜合性企業集團。公司按照「產城創新建
                                                設者、幸福民生運營商」的定位，以物業開發、物業投資及城市綜合運營服務為
  Shum Yip Luxury Mansion                       核心業務，同時涉足高科農業、高端製造等創新業務，致力於轉型升級成以城
         深業頤樾府                                  市綜合開發運營、科技產業的投資服務為主業的科技型產業集團。

                                                公 司 深 耕 深 圳，聚 焦 粵 港 澳 大 灣 區，佈 局 長 三 角 區 域 及 重 點 省 會 等 高 能 級 城
                                                市，目前本集團於國內多個城市合共擁有儲備建築面積約622萬平方米的土地
                                                儲備，其中位於粵港澳大灣區的優質土地儲備的建築面積約413萬平方米。公司
                                                致力優化土地儲備的結構，將資產和業務向粵港澳大灣區和重點區域的高能級
                                                城市集中。

                                                公司一貫之核心策略目標，乃致力推動業務長期持續發展，以期為股東帶來整
                                                體穩定回報及締造價值增長。

                                                                                  Shum Yip Cloud Center
                                                                                        深業雲築




                                                                     “Promoting stability through
                                                                    progress and fostering
                                                                  transformation through innovation
                                                                 with integrity”
                                                      「以進促穩謀發展，守正創新促轉型」



In 2024, amid complex and severe circumstances of increasing external pressure         2024年，國內經濟面對外部壓力加大、內部困難增多
and internal challenges, China’s economy maintained overall stability with steady      的複雜嚴峻形勢，經濟運行總體平穩、穩中有進。面
progress. In the face of incessant market challenges, the Group rose to the            對市場帶來的多重挑戰，集團迎難而上，主動作為，
occasion courageously and took proactive measures, achieving solid progress            扎實推動各項工作取得成效，地產銷售積極去化，運
in various sectors: our sales of real estate accelerated notably, operational          營拓展成效凸顯，創新業務蓄勢待發，轉型發展縱深
expansion results was outstanding, innovation business gained momentum for             推進。在房地產市場持續調整的大環境下，本集團全
greater breakthrough, and the transformation of the Company was advanced               年實現營業收入155.7億港元，毛利44.4億港元。受毛
steadily in depth. Against the background of continuous adjustment of the real         利率下降影響，本集團核心盈利約為9.5億港元，疊加
estate market, the Group achieved an annual revenue of HK$15.57 billion, with          存貨計提減值、投資物業公允價值減少、聯合營公司
a gross profit of HK$4.44 billion. However, due to the contraction of gross profit     業 績 虧 損 及 投 資 撥 備 的 因 素，本 公 司 權 益 股 東 應 佔
margin, the Group recorded core earnings of approximately HK$950 million.              虧損18.2億港元。經審慎考慮業績短期波動和現金流
Coupled with the factors including provision for impairment of inventories,            情況，董事會認為需預留足夠資金應對行業風險和機
decrease in fair value of investment properties, losses from associates and joint      遇，不建議派發2024年末期股息。
ventures and investment provision, loss attributable to the equity shareholders
of the Company was HK$1.82 billion. After prudent consideration of short-term
performance fluctuations and our cash flow conditions, the Board is of the view
that it is essential to retain funds to address industry risks and opportunities and
thus does not recommend the payment of a final dividend for 2024.

 Focusing on operations while striving to promote the stable                         聚焦經營，推動企業穩健發展
 development of the Company
 Our real estate business progressed steadily                                        地產業務穩步推進
 The Group actively advanced its real estate business while strengthening control    集團積極推進地產業務，強化項目關鍵節點管控，年
 of the key nodes of its projects, with 11 projects completed during the year, and   內11個項目完成竣備，開發業務結轉收入約91.3億港
 income recognized from the property development business of approximately           元。銷售方面，集團搶抓政策窗口期加速去化，全年
 HK$9.13 billion. In terms of sales of properties, the Group seized policy           實 現 地 產 簽 約 銷 售 近169.7億 元 人 民 幣（約186.1億 港
 windows to accelerate the destocking process, achieving contracted property         元），較去年下降36.2%。多個項目取得優異成績，上
 sales of nearly RMB16.97 billion (approximately HK$18.61 billion) for the year,     城學府開盤三小時即售罄，成為深圳「 9 · 29新政」後首
 representing a decrease of 36.2% as compared to last year. Numerous projects        個「日光盤」；上林苑公寓產品開盤勁銷超七成，再造

 城學府）was sold out within three hours after launch, becoming the first “Day-          成都的項目也取得可觀的銷售成績。建設方面，集團

Our operation business expanded rapidly                                                運營業務加速拓展
The spin-off and listing of urban integrated operation business achieved results,      城市綜合運營業務分拆上市工作取得成效，業務拓展
and a historic breakthrough was made in business expansion, with contracted            取 得 歷 史 性 突 破，合 同 簽 約 面 積 超1億 平 方 米，全 年
GFA exceeding 100 million sq.m. and a net expanded contracted area of                  淨拓展合同面積約1,539萬平方米。年內，集團物業管
approximately 15.39 million sq.m. for the year. In 2024, the Group’s property          理收入約32.9億港元，較去年上漲17.8%。城市服務方
management revenue amounted to approximately HK$3.29 billion, representing             面，深業運營首進香港腹地，承接廣深港高鐵西九龍
an increase of 17.8% over last year. In respect of city services, Shum Yip             站內地口岸區管養服務；物管城市再擴版圖，承接深

Corporate Governance Practices                                                      企業管治常規
The Company is committed to maintaining high standard of corporate                  本公司致力恪守奉行高標準之企業管治。董事會（「董
governance. The board of Directors (the “Board”) strongly believes that good        事會」）堅信，良好企業管治對有效之管理、健全之企
corporate governance is essential for effective management, a healthy corporate     業文化、成功之業務發展，以及提升股東價值至關重
culture, successful business growth and enhancing shareholders’ value. The          要。本公司已於整個2024年應用並遵守香港聯合交易
Company has applied the principles and complied with all code provisions of the     所有限公司證券上市規則（「上市規則」）附錄C1所載之
Corporate Governance Code (“CG Code”) as set out in Appendix C1 to the Rules        企業管治守則（「企業管治守則」）之所有原則及守則
Governing the Listing of Securities on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited      條文。
(“Listing Rules”) throughout the year 2024.

 Remuneration Committee                                                               薪酬委員會
 The Remuneration Committee comprises three members, all being Independent            薪酬委員會由三名成員（均為獨立非執行董事）組成。
 Non-executive Directors. It is chaired by Dr. WONG Yau Kar, David and other          薪酬委員會由黃友嘉博士擔任主席，其他成員為李偉
 members are Mr. LI Wai Keung and Prof. GONG Peng.                                    強先生及宮鵬教授。

 The Company adopted the model whereby the Remuneration Committee is                  本公司已採納由薪酬委員會負責根據其職權範圍就
 responsible for making recommendation to the Board on the remuneration               個別執行董事及高級管理人員的薪酬待遇向董事會
 packages of individual Executive Director and senior management according to its     提出建議的模式。薪酬委員會亦負責就非執行董事的
 terms of reference. The Remuneration Committee is also responsible for making        薪酬事項向董事會提出建議、設立恰當及透明的程序
 recommendation to the Board on the remuneration matters of Non-executive             以制訂薪酬、根據上市規則第17章審閱及╱或批准購
 Directors, the setting up of adequate and transparent procedures for setting         股權計劃相關事項。薪酬委員會的職權範圍全文可於
 such remuneration, reviewing and/or approving matters relating to share option       本公司網站及聯交所網站內查閱。
 schemes under Chapter 17 of the Listing Rules. The full terms of reference of the
 Remuneration Committee are available on the Company’s website and the Stock
 Exchange’s website.

 The Group’s Remuneration Policy aims to ensure that the remuneration level is        本集團的薪酬政策旨在確保薪酬水平適當並與本公
 appropriate and align with the Company’s goals, objectives and performance,          司的目標、宗旨及表現保持一致，以便招聘及挽留優
 so as to facilitate the recruitment and retention of outstanding directors, senior   秀董事、高級管理人員及員工，並為彼等提供充足動
 management and employees and to provide them with adequate incentive                 力以達成業務目標。於釐定董事及僱員薪酬時，須考
 for achieving business targets. When determining Directors’ and employees’           慮包括整體市場及房地產行業薪酬水平；相關職位及
 remuneration, factors including salary levels in the market as a whole and in        工作領域的市場基準；本集團及個人的表現在內的因
 the real estate industry; market benchmarks for relevant positions and work          素。亦將根據個人表現及本集團業績向僱員提供花紅
 areas; the performance of the Group and the individual etc. shall be considered.     及其他獎勵（包括購股權）。此外，薪酬方案每年進行
 Bonuses and other incentives including share options will also be provided to        一次審閱，以確保內部公平及競爭力。
 employees based on individual performance and the results of the Group. And,
 remuneration packages are reviewed on an annual basis to ensure internal equity
 and competitiveness.

 No Director or any of his/her associates is allowed to determine their own           任何董事或其任何聯繫人士不得自行釐訂其本身的
 remuneration. In making recommendation to the Board on the remuneration of           薪 酬。在 向 董 事 會 建 議 董 事 及 高 級 管 理 人 員 的 薪 酬
 Directors and senior management, the Remuneration Committee will take into           時，薪酬委員會將考慮本集團薪酬政策所載的因素。
 account the factors set out in the Group’s Remuneration Policy.

 During the year, the Remuneration Committee reviewed the remuneration of             年內，薪酬委員會已審議董事，包括新委任執行董事
 the Directors, including the remuneration of newly appointed Executive Director,     及高級管理層之薪酬。薪酬委員會亦檢討了本集團的
 and senior management. The Remuneration Committee also reviewed the overall          整體薪酬架構，並審議了各種以表現為基準的激勵安
 remuneration structure of the Group and considered the forms and adequacy of         排（包括購股權）的形式及充足性。
 the various performance-based incentive arrangements including share options.

Risk Management and Internal Control                                                風險管理與內部監控
Responsibility                                                                      責任
The Board is responsible for the risk management and internal control systems       董事會須對風險管理及內部監控系統負責，有責任檢
and reviewing the effectiveness of such systems. Management is responsible for      討該等制度的有效性，而管理層則負責設計及執行內
designing and implementing an internal control system to manage the risks to the    部監控制度以最大限度的管理公司面臨的風險，達到
Company to the maximum extent, in order to identify and manage these risks so       識別和管理這些風險，從而可以掌握、降低、舒緩、轉
as to understand, reduce, mitigate, divert or avoid these risks. The Board hereby   移或規避這些風險。董事會謹在此闡釋該等系統旨在
explains that the systems are designed to manage, rather than eliminate, the risk   管理而非消除未能達成業務目標的風險，而且只能就
of failure to meet business objectives, and only to provide reasonable, but not     不會有重大的失實陳述或損失作出合理而非絕對的
absolute, assurance against material misstatement or loss.                          保證。

Risk Management Structure                                                           風險管理架構

 Board Oversight                                                                    董事會監督
 Audit Committee, acting on behalf of the Board                                     代表董事會行事的審核委員會
 •     Ensure that an appropriate and effective risk management framework is        •   確保公司已設立及維持一個合適和有效的
       established and maintained by the Company.                                       風險管理架構。

 •     Oversee management in the design, implementation and monitoring of           •   監督管理層在風險管理架構的設計、執行及
       the risk management framework.                                                   監察方面的工作。

 •     Oversee management in their risk mitigation efforts.                         •   監督管理層在風險紓緩方面的工作。

 Risk Management, Reporting and Communication                                       風險管理、匯報及溝通

       the establishment and operation of the comprehensive risk management             理體系建設與運作的具體工作；指導、督促
       system of the Company; guide and supervise the overall risk                      其他各部門及各所屬企業開展全面風險管
       management of other departments and the respective enterprises which             理工作。
       they belong to.

 •     Risk management: regularly conduct systematic risk evaluation and            •   風險管理：定期進行系統風險評估、對重大
       carry out risk audit to major businesses in advance.                             業務進行事前風險審核。
 •     Internal control: inspect, evaluate and optimize the implementation of       •   內部控制：檢查、評價和優化內控制度的執
       internal control system, to avoid execution risks.                               行情況，規避執行上的風險。

 •     Internal audit: conduct multi-level audit, implement auditing issues         •   內部審計：開展多層次的審計工作，落實審
       rectification work, prevent operational risks.                                   計問題整改，防範營運風險。

 •     Ensure that a review of the effectiveness of the risk management             •   確保至少每年檢討一次風險管理架構的成
       framework has been conducted at least annually and provide such                  效，並透過審核委員會向董事會作出有關確
       confirmation to the Board through the Audit Committee.                           認。

 •     Optimize dynamic monitoring and early warning of risks to facilitate         •   完善動態風險監測與預警，每月每季度推進
       inspection and tracking monitoring of potential risk hazards on a                風險隱患排查和跟蹤監測，建立健全公司風
       monthly and quarterly basis, meanwhile, establish, and effectively               險防範化解機制，並有效落實執行。
       implement, a sound risk prevention and resolution mechanism for the
       Company.

 Risk Management Process                                                                 風險管理程序
 The Company adopts an integrated “bottom-up” and “top-down” risk review                 公司採用「由下而上」的方式配合「由上而下」的綜合風
 process, requiring the heads of functional departments and business units of the        險檢討程序，要求公司職能部門及業務部門主管參與
 Company to participate in identifying operational risks and incorporate them into       識別營運風險，納入各項業務及決策流程中，包括策
 various business and decision-making processes, including strategy formulation,         略制定、業務發展、業務規劃、資金分配、投資決定、
 business development, business planning, capital allocation, investment                 內部監控及日常營運等，以便於
 decisions, internal controls and daily operations, to enable

 (1)   comprehensive identification and prioritization of all significant risks within   (1)   全面識別公司內部所有的重大風險，並進行優
       the Company,                                                                            次排序，

 (2)   reporting of significant risks to the appropriate management level,               (2)   將重大風險上報至適當的管理層級別，

 (3)   the management to effectively communicate about the risks.                        (3)   讓管理層就風險進行有效溝通。

 Internal Auditing                                                                           內部審計
 The risk management department is responsible for the internal audit of the                 風險管理部門負責公司內部審計，運用其設立的《全
 Company. It reviews the risks involved in daily operations of the Company                   面風險管理手冊》及《內部審計管理辦法》中清晰列明
 and its subsidiaries by using the audit plans and procedures set out in the                 的審核計劃及程序，檢討公司及附屬企業的日常運營
 “Comprehensive Risk Management Manual” and the “Management Measures for                     面臨的風險，並對內部審計項目做定期後評價檢討以
 Internal Auditing” it established, and conducts regular post-evaluation reviews on          保證風險管理架構的成效。
 internal audit items to ensure the effectiveness of risk management framework.

 Each year, the risk management department determines the internal audit work                風險管理部每年度按照公司戰略部署，根據經營管理
 priorities and formulates annual internal audit plans according to the Company’s            需 要 和 公 司 的 年 度 工 作 安 排，確 定 內 部 審 計 工 作 重
 strategic deployment, business management needs and annual work schedule.                   點，制定年度內部審計計劃。

 The annual internal audit plan shall include the following basic elements:                  年度內部審計計劃當包括以下基本內容：

 1)   Annual work objectives of internal audit;                                              1)     內部審計年度工作目標；

 3)   The audit resources allocated to each audit item.                                      3)     各審計項目所分配的審計資源。

                    operation and economic benefits is required.

 1    General information                                                          1     一般資料
      Shenzhen Investment Limited (the “Company”) is a limited liability company         深 圳 控 股 有 限 公 司（「本 公 司」）為 一 間 於 香 港
      incorporated in Hong Kong. The address of the Company’s registered office          註冊成立之有限責任公司。本公司之註冊辦事
      is located at 8th Floor, New East Ocean Centre, 9 Science Museum Road,             處位於香港九龍科學館道9號新東海商業中心8
      Kowloon, Hong Kong. The Company is an investment holding company. The              樓。本公司為一間投資控股公司。本公司及其附
      Company and its subsidiaries (collectively referred to as the “Group”) are         屬公司（統稱為「本集團」）主要從事物業發展、
      principally engaged in property development, property investment, property         物業投資、物業管理及生產運營。本集團合營公
      management and manufacture operation. The principal activities of the              司及聯營公司的主要業務披露於附註18及19。
      Group’s joint ventures and associates are disclosed in notes 18 and 19.

      In the opinion of the directors, the immediate holding company of the              董事認為，本公司之直接控股公司為於香港註
      Company is Shum Yip Holdings Company Limited (“Shum Yip Holdings”, 深               冊成立之私人公司－深業（集團）有限公司（「深業
      業（集團）有限公司）, which is a private company incorporated in Hong                        （集團）」）。本公司之最終控股公司為於中華人
      Kong. The ultimate holding company of the Company is 深業集團有限公                       民共和國（「中國」）深圳成立之國有公司－深業
      司 (“Shum Yip Group”), which is a state-owned company established in                集團有限公司（「深業集團」）。
      Shenzhen, the People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”).

      The Company’s shares are listed on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong                 本公司股份於香港聯合交易所有限公司上市。
      Limited.

      The consolidated financial information is presented in Hong Kong dollar            除另有指明者外，綜合財務資料乃以港元（「港
      (“HK$”) and all values are rounded to the nearest thousand (HK$’000)               元」）呈 列，所 有 金 額 約 整 至 最 接 近 千 位（千 港
      except when otherwise indicated.                                                   元）。

      These consolidated financial statements have been approved for issue by            該等綜合財務報表已於2025年3月28日獲董事會
      the Board of Directors on 28 March 2025.                                           批准刊發。

 2    Basic of preparation                                                         2     編製基準
 2.1 Going concern basis                                                           2.1   持續經營基準
     The directors of the Company have given careful consideration to the                本公司董事在評估本集團2024年12月31日後至
     future liquidity and performance of the Group and its available sources             少12個 月 是 否 有 充 足 財 務 資 源 繼 續 持 續 經 營
     of financing in assessing whether the Group will have sufficient financial          時，已審慎考慮本集團的未來流動資金及表現
     resources to continue as a going concern for at least of 12 months from 31          以及其可用的融資資源。
     December 2024.

      entity will exercise its right to defer settlement. The amendments also                  清，負債可以其本身的權益工具結算，且僅當可

     The Group’s major operations are mainly located in the PRC and is                        本集團之主要業務大部份位於中國，受特定因
     subject to special considerations and significant risks. These include                   素影響，承受重大風險。該等風險包括（其中包

     environment, influence of national authorities over pricing regulation and               控的影響以及市場競爭。
     competition in the market.

     The Group’s activities expose it to a variety of financial risks: market risk            本 集 團 因 其 業 務 活 動 面 臨 各 種 財 務 風 險：市
     (mainly including currency risk, interest rate risk and price risk), credit risk         場 風 險（主 要 包 括 貨 幣 風 險、利 率 風 險 及 價 格
     and liquidity risk. The Group’s overall risk management programme focuses                風險）、信貸風險及流動性風險。本集團之整體
     on the unpredictability of financial markets and seeks to minimise potential             風險管理計劃針對金融市場不可預測之特性，

           for irrecoverable amounts.                                                       撥備。

         occurs, the defined standards adopted by the Group are consistent                    否出現信貸減值，本集團於考慮定性及定

         indicators. When the Group assesses whether the debtor has credit                    債務人是否出現信貸減值時，主要考慮下

               payment date.                                                                       過90日。

               where such concessions are normally reluctant to be made by                         款人於一般情況下不願意授出的寬
               the lender.                                                                         免。

3.2 Capital management                                                              3.2   資本管理
    The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to safeguard the Group’s             本集團管理資本目標為維持本集團持續經營能
    ability to continue as a going concern, to provide returns for shareholders           力，以為股東提供回報為其他利益相關方創造
    and benefits for other stakeholders and to maintain an optimal capital                利益，並保持最佳資本結構以減低資金成本。
    structure to reduce the cost of capital.

     In order to optimise the capital structure, the Group may adjust the amount          為優化資本架構，本集團可能會調整已向股東
     of dividends paid to shareholders, repurchase of shares, issue new shares            支 付 的 股 息 金 額、回 購 股 份、發 行 新 股 或 出 售
     or sell assets.                                                                      資產。

     Consistent with other companies in the market, the Group monitors its                本集團透過審閱其負債率監察其資本，與市場
     capital by reviewing its gearing ratio. This ratio is calculated as net debt         上其他公司一致。負債率乃按債務淨額除以本
     divided by equity attributable to owners of the Company. Net debt is                 公司權益股東應佔權益計算。淨債務按借款總額
     calculated as total borrowings (including bank and other borrowings as               （包括綜合財務狀況表內所示銀行及其他借貸）
     shown in the consolidated statement of financial position) less cash and             減去現金及現金等價物、受限制現金及銀行存
     cash equivalents, restricted cash and bank deposits.                                 款計算。

     The gearing ratio as at 31 December 2024 and 2023 were as follows:                   於2024年及2023年12月31日的負債率如下：

       Less: Cash and cash equivalents (note 26)      減：現金及現金等價物（附註26 ）                               (5,786,000)           (11,053,858)
             Restricted cash and bank deposits          受限制現金及銀行存款（附註26 ）

      •     Quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets for identical assets              •   同等資產或負債之活躍市場報價（未經調
            or liabilities. The quoted prices already incorporates the market’s                整）。所報價格已納入市場對經濟環境變化
            assumptions with respect to changes in economic climate such                       （如 利 率 上 升 及 通 脹）以 及 因 環 境、社 會
            as rising interest rates and inflation, as well as changes due to                  及管治（「ESG 」）風險所導致變化的假設（第
            Environmental, Social and Governance (“ESG”) risks (level 1)                       一級）

      •     Inputs other than quoted prices included within level 1 that are               •   除第一級所包括報價外，資產或負債之可
            observable for the assets or liabilities, either directly (that is, as             觀察輸入值，可為直接（即例如價格）或間
            prices) or indirectly (that is, derived from prices) (level 2)                     接（即源自價格）
                                                                                                      （第二級）

      •     Inputs for the assets or liabilities that are not based on observable          •   就非上市股權證券及ESG風險會導致重大
            market data (that is, unobservable inputs). This is the case for                   不可觀察調整的工具而言，其資產或負債
            unlisted equity securities and for instruments where ESG risk gives                之輸入值並非基於可觀察市場數據（即不
            rise to a significant unobservable adjustment (level 3)                            可觀察輸入值）

 (c) Impairment of non-financial assets                                                 (c)   非金融資產之減值
     Where an indication of impairment exists, or when annual impairment                      如有跡象顯示出現減值或須就資產進行年度
     testing for an asset is required (other than inventories, contract assets,               減值測試（不包括存貨、合約資產、遞延稅項資
     deferred tax assets, financial assets, and investment properties), the                   產、金融資產及投資物業），則會估計資產的可
     asset’s recoverable amount is estimated. An asset’s recoverable amount                   收回金額。資產的可收回金額按資產或現金產
     is the higher of the asset’s or cash-generating unit’s value in use and                  生單位的使用價值或公允值（以較高者為準）減
     its fair value less costs of disposal, and is determined for an individual               銷售成本而計量，並就個別資產而釐定，除非有
     asset, unless the asset does not generate cash inflows that are largely                  關資產並不產生在很大程度上獨立於其他資產
     independent of those from other assets or groups of assets, in which case                或資產組別的現金流入，則在此情況下，會就資
     the recoverable amount is determined for the cash-generating unit to which               產所屬現金產生單位釐定可收回金額。
     the asset belongs.

      An impairment loss is recognised only if the carrying amount of an asset                減值虧損僅於資產賬面值超過其可收回金額時
      exceeds its recoverable amount. In assessing value in use, the estimated                確認。於評估使用價值時，估計未來現金流量按
      future cash flows are discounted to their present value using a pre-tax                 可反映現時市場對貨幣時間價值及資產特定風
      discount rate that reflects current market assessments of the time value of             險的評估的稅前貼現率貼現至現值。減值虧損
      money and the risks specific to the asset. An impairment loss is charged                乃於產生期間自損益表與減值資產功能相符的
      to the statement of profit or loss in the period in which it arises in those            相關開支類別扣除。
      expense categories consistent with the function of the impaired asset.

      An assessment is made at the end of each reporting period as to whether                 於各報告期末均會評估是否有任何跡象顯示過
      there is an indication that previously recognised impairment losses may                 往確認的減值虧損不再存在或可能已經減少。
      no longer exist or may have decreased. If such an indication exists, the                倘出現任何上述跡象，則會估計可收回金額。先
      recoverable amount is estimated. A previously recognised impairment loss                前就商譽以外的資產所確認的減值虧損僅於用
      of an asset other than goodwill is reversed only if there has been a change             作釐定該資產的可收回金額的估計有變動時，

      but not to an amount higher than the carrying amount that would have                    並無確認該資產的減值虧損的情況下，該資產
      been determined (net of any depreciation/amortisation) had no impairment                應有的賬面值（扣除任何折舊╱攤銷）。撥回該
      loss been recognised for the asset in prior years. A reversal of such an                等減值虧損將計入其產生期間的損益表內。
      impairment loss is credited to the statement of profit or loss in the period in
      which it arises.

(b) Accounting policies of revenue recognition                                      (b)   收益確認之會計政策
    Income is classified by the Group as revenue when it arises from the sale of          當本集團於日常業務過程中提供服務或他人根
    goods, the provision of services or the use by others of the Group’s assets           據租賃使用本集團資產而產生銷售貨品收入
    under leases in the ordinary course of the Group’s business.                          時，本集團將收入分類為收益。

     Revenue is recognised when control over a product or service is transferred          當產品或服務之控制權轉移至客戶或承租人有
     to the customer, or the lessee has the right to use the asset, at the amount         權使用資產時，按本集團預期將享有的承諾代
     of promised consideration to which the Group is expected to be entitled,             價金額確認收益，惟不包括代第三方收取的款
     excluding those amounts collected on behalf of third parties. Revenue                項。收益不包括增值稅或其他銷售稅，並經扣減
     excludes value added tax or other sales taxes and is after deduction of any          任何貿易折扣後計算。
     trade discounts.

 (b) Accounting policies of revenue recognition (continued)                             (b)   收益確認之會計政策（續）
     Where the contract contains a financing component which provides a                       倘合約載有向客戶提供超過12個月的重大融資
     significant financing benefit to the customer for more than 12 months,                   利益的融資組成部份，則收益按應收金額的現
     revenue is measured at the present value of the amount receivable,                       值計量，並使用反映於與有關客戶的個別融資
     discounted using the discount rate that would be reflected in a separate                 交易的折現率貼現，且利息收入根據實際利率
     financing transaction with the customer, and interest income is accrued                  法分開累計。倘合約載有向本集團提供重大融
     separately under the effective interest method. Where the contract contains              資利益的融資組成部份，則根據該合約確認的
     a financing component which provides a significant financing benefit to                  收益包括根據實際利率法合約責任附有的利息
     the Group, revenue recognised under that contract includes the interest                  開支。
     expense accreted on the contract liability under the effective interest
     method.

      The Group applies advantage of the practical expedient in paragraph 63                  本集團利用香港財務報告準則第15號第63段中
      of HKFRS 15 and does not adjust the consideration for any effects of a                  的 實 務 簡 便 做 法，並 無 就 融 資 期 為12個 月 或 以
      significant financing component if the period of financing is 12 months or              下的重大融資組成部份的任何影響調整對價。
      less.

      (i)     Sales of properties                                                             (i)     物業銷售
              Revenue from sales of properties is recognised upon the point in time                   當物業控制權轉移至客戶時，即物業建造
              when the control of the properties is transferred to the customers,                     已完成、物業已交付予客戶及相關應收款
              which is when the construction of properties has been completed,                        項之可收回性有合理保證時，按時點以確
              the properties have been delivered to the customers and the                             認銷售物業的收益。於收益確認日期前就
              recoverability of related receivables is reasonably assured. Deposits                   出售的物業收取的訂金及分期款項計入財
              and instalments received on properties sold prior to the date of                        務狀況表內合約負債項下。
              revenue recognition are included in the statement of financial position
              under contract liabilities.

      (ii)    Sales of goods                                                                  (ii)    銷售貨品
              Revenue is recognised when the customer takes possession of and                         收益於客戶佔有並接納產品時確認。
              accepts the products.

      (iii)   Construction income                                                             (iii)   建造收入
              A contract with a customer is classified by the Group as a                              當合約與受客戶管控的房地產資產工程相
              construction contract when the contract relates to work on real                         關時，本集團將與客戶所訂的合約列作建
              estate assets under the control of the customer and therefore the                       造合約，故本集團建造活動設置或提升受
              Group’s construction activities create or enhance an asset under the                    客戶管控的資產。
              customer’s control.

              When the outcome of a construction contract can be reasonably                           當建造合約的結果能合理計量時，合約收
              measured, revenue from the contract is recognised progressively over                    益採用成本法（即根據所產生的實際成本
              time using the cost-to-cost method, i.e. based on the proportion of                     佔估計總成本的比例）隨時間累計確認。
              the actual costs incurred relative to the estimated total costs.

     (iv)   Rental income from leases                                                   (iv)   租賃的租金收入

            term, except where an alternative basis is more representative of                  更能代表使用租賃資產所產生的收益模式
            the pattern of benefits to be derived from the use of the leased                   時除外。所授租賃優惠作為應收租賃款項
            asset. Lease incentives granted are recognised in profit or loss as                淨額總額的組成部份在損益內確認。不依
            an integral part of the aggregate net lease payments receivable.                   賴於指數或利率之可變租賃付款按賺取租
            Variable lease payments that do not depend on an index or a rate                   金的會計期間的收入確認。
            are recognised as income in the accounting period in which they are
            earned.

     (v)    Property management service income                                          (v)    物業管理服務收入
            The Group provides property management services and bills the                      本集團提供物業管理服務，且業主須按月
            property owners a fixed lump sum amount for services provided                      就所提供的服務支付固定包乾費用。根據
            on a monthly basis. Under this arrangement, the Group acts as                      該安排，本集團作為主事人主要負責向業

            when the Group has a right to invoice and that corresponds directly                時，本集團將已收或應收業主的費用確認
            with the value of performance completed, and all related property                  為其收益。
            management costs as its cost of services.

(b) Income from demolition and relocation                                            (b)   拆遷收入
    During the year 2024, the Group entered into a relocation compensation                 於2024年 度，本 集 團 與 深 圳 市 福 升 建 設 開 發 有
    and resettlement agreement with Shenzhen Fusheng Construction and                      限公司就位於深圳市福田區園嶺街道Gualiaoling

    of the urban renewal unit. The agreement stipulates that the Group’s                   將以安置物業作為補償。本集團已參照將收到
    demolished properties will be compensated with relocation properties. The              的代價的公允值確認拆遷處置收益。
    Group has recognized the disposal gains from demolition with reference to
    the fair value of the consideration to be received.

    costs and all property development expenditures.                                     發支出在內之可扣減開支。

   of the PRC subsidiaries are qualified as tax residents of Hong Kong                   用較低的5%預扣稅率。本公司的適用股息預扣
   according to the tax treat arrangements between the PRC and Hong Kong.                稅率為5%。

15 Investment properties (continued)                                                 15 投資物業（續）
(a) Fair value hierarchy (continued)                                                 (a) 公允值等級架構（續）
    The fair value of the Group’s investment properties is determined using            本集團之投資物業公允值採用收入資本化法及
    income capitalisation method and residual method.                                  剩餘法予以釐定。

     Income capitalisation method determines fair values by discounting the            收入資本化法透過採用資本化率折現有關物業
     projected cash flows associated with the properties using capitalisation          之預測現金流量釐定公允值。有關估值考慮到
     rates. The valuation takes into account the expected market rental and            各物業之預期市場租金及空置率。所用貼現率
     vacancy rate of the respective properties. The discount rates used have           乃就樓宇的質素及地點以及租戶信貸質素作出
     been adjusted for the quality and location of the buildings and the tenant        調整。公允值計量與預期市場租金正相關，惟與
     credit quality. The fair value measurement is positively correlated to the        空置率及資本化率負相關。
     expected market rental, and negatively correlated to the vacancy rate and
     the capitalisation rate.

     The residual method takes into account the residual value of the gross            剩餘法考慮總發展價值並扣除尚未發生的建築
     development value (“GDV”) after deduction of the outstanding construction         成本、開支以及發展利潤後的剩餘價值。剩餘法
     costs and expenses as well as profit element. It involves firstly the             首先計算假設建議發展項目於估值日已落成的
     assessment of the aggregate GDV of the proposed development, as if                合計總發展價值。建議發展項目的估計成本包
     completed, as at the valuation date. Estimated costs of the proposed              括建築成本、市場推廣支出、專業費用、財務費
     development include construction costs, marketing expense, professional           用以及相關成本，另加開發商的風險及利潤撥
     fee, finance charges and associated costs, plus an allowance for the              備。尚未發生的開發成本將由總發展價值中扣
     developer’s risk and profit. The outstanding development costs are then           除，並得出標的物業的剩餘價值。
     deducted from the GDV and the resultant figure is the residual value of the
     subject property.

(b) Undiscounted lease payments under non-cancellable operating                      (b) 不可取消經營租賃之未貼現租賃付款
    leases
    The Group leases out its investment properties under operating leases. The         本集團根據經營租賃出租其投資物業。租約一
    leases typically run for an initial period of 1 to 20 years, with an option to     般初步為期1至20年，並有權於到期日續租，同
    renew the leases after the date at which time all terms are renegotiated.          時重新商討所有條款。租賃付款一般每年增加
    Lease payments are usually increased annually to reflect market rentals.           一次，以反映市場租金。本集團若干租約包含基
    Certain leases of the group contain variable lease payment terms that are          於租戶收益的可變租賃付款條款。
    based on the revenue of tenants.

 (c) Accounting policies of Investment properties                                     (c)   投資物業之會計政策
     Investment properties, principally freehold office buildings, are held for             投資物業主要為終身保有寫字樓，乃為長期租
     long-term rental yields and are not occupied by the Group. Investment                  金 收 益 而 持 有，並 非 由 本 集 團 佔 用。投 資 物 業
     property is initially measured at cost, including related transaction costs            初始按成本計量，包括相關交易成本及借款成本
     and where applicable borrowing costs. Subsequently, they are carried at fair           （如適用）。其後按公允值計量。
     value.

      Fair value is based on active market prices, adjusted, if necessary, for any          公允值以活躍市場價格為基準，並在有需要的
      difference in the nature, location or condition of the specific asset. If the         情況下根據特定資產之性質、地點或條件之任
      information is not available, the Group uses alternative valuation methods            何差別調整公允值。倘無法取得此等資料，本集
      such as recent prices on less active markets. Changes in fair values are              團會採用其他估值方法，例如活躍度較低市場
      presented in profit or loss.                                                          之近期價格。公允值變動於損益內呈列。

15 Investment properties (continued)                                                 15    投資物業（續）
(c) Accounting policies of Investment properties (continued)                         (c)   投資物業之會計政策（續）
    If an item of owner-occupied property becomes an investment property                   倘擁有人佔用物業項目因用途改變而成為投資
    because its use has changed, any difference resulting between the carrying             物業，則該項目於轉讓日期的賬面值與公允值
    amount and the fair value of this item at the date of transfer is treated in           之間產生的差額按與根據香港會計準則第16號
    the same way as a revaluation under HKAS 16. Any resulting increase in the             重估相同的方式處理。該物業產生的任何賬面
    carrying amount of the property is recognised in profit or loss to the extent          值增長於損益中確認，惟須撥回之前減值虧損，
    that it reverses a previous impairment loss, with any remaining increase               任何剩餘增長於其他全面收益中確認，並直接
    recognised in other comprehensive income and increase directly to equity               撥入權益中的重估盈餘。該物業產生的任何賬
    in revaluation surplus within equity. Any resulting decrease in the carrying           面值減少首先於其他全面收益中自先前確認的
    amount of the property is initially charged in other comprehensive income              任何重估盈餘中扣除，而任何剩餘減值於損益
    against any previously recognised revaluation surplus, with any remaining              中扣除。對於由持作待售之已落成物業或發展
    decrease charged to profit or loss. For a transfer from completed properties           中物業轉為將按公允值列賬的投資物業，該日
    held for sale or properties under development to investment properties that            物業公允值與其過往賬面值的任何差額於損益
    will be carried at fair value, any difference between the fair value of the            內確認。
    property at that date and its previous carrying amount shall be recognised
    in profit or loss.

     The Group shall transfer a property from investment properties to properties          當一項物業啟動旨在出售的相關開發時，本集
     under development when it commences related development with a view to                團將投資物業轉為發展中物業。對於按公允值
     sale. For a transfer from investment properties that is carried at fair value         列賬的投資物業轉為發展中物業，相關發展物
     to properties under development, related properties under development                 業須按轉撥日期的公允值確認。
     shall be recognised at fair value at the date of transfer.

     The Group classifies cash outflows to acquire or construct investment                 本集團將收購或建造投資物業的現金流出分類
     property as investing and rental inflows as operating cash flows.                     為投資性現金流量，而租金流入則分類為經營
                                                                                           現金流量。

     Shum King. As the investment in Shum King mainly relates to the loans               資主要與貸款有關，而貸款被視為投資的一部

(i)   Accounting policies of financial assets                                        (i)   金融資產會計政策
      (a) Classification                                                                   (a) 分類
          The Group classifies its financial assets in the following measurement               本集團將其金融資產按以下計量類別分
          categories:                                                                          類：

           •     those to be measured subsequently at fair value (either through             •     其後按公允值計量（計入其他全面收
                 other comprehensive income (“OCI”), or through profit or loss),                   益（「其他全面收益」）或計入損益）的
                 and                                                                               金融資產；及

           •     those to be measured at amortised cost.                                     •     將按攤銷成本計量的金融資產。

           The classification depends on the entity’s business model for                     該分類取決於實體管理金融資產及合約現
           managing the financial assets and the contractual terms of the cash               金流量年期之業務模式。
           flows.

           For assets measured at fair value, gains and losses will either be                就按公允值計量之資產而言，收益及虧損
           recorded in profit or loss or OCI. For investments in debt instruments,           將於損益或其他全面收益入賬。就於債務
           this will depend on the business model in which the investment                    工具的投資而言，將視乎持有投資的業務
           is held. For investments in equity instruments, this will depend on               模式而定。對於權益工具投資，這將取決
           whether the Group has made an irrevocable election at the time of                 於本集團在初始確認時是否作出不可撤銷
           initial recognition to account for the equity investment at FVOCI.                的選擇而將其作為透過其他全面收益按公
                                                                                             允值計算之股權投資入賬。

           The Group reclassifies debt investments when and only when its                    僅當管理該等資產之業務模式發生變動
           business model for managing those assets changes.                                 時，本 集 團 才 會 對 債 務 投 資 進 行 重 新 分
                                                                                             類。

           Recognition and derecognition                                                     確認及終止確認
           Regular way purchases and sales of financial assets are recognised                常規購買及出售的金融資產在交易日確
           on trade-date, the date on which the Group commits to purchase or                 認，交易日指本集團承諾購買或出售該資
           sell the asset. Financial assets are derecognised when the rights to              產之日。當從金融資產收取現金流量的權
           receive cash flows from the financial assets have expired or have                 利已到期或已轉讓，而本集團已實質上將
           been transferred and the Group has transferred substantially all the              所有權的所有風險和報酬轉讓時，金融資
           risks and rewards of ownership.                                                   產即終止確認。

 29 Share option scheme                                                              29     購股權計劃
      The Company operated a share option scheme (the “Scheme”) which was                      本公司設有一項購股權計劃（「計劃」）於2012年
      approved and adopted on 22 June 2012 for the purpose of providing                        6月22日批准及採納，以對本集團業務成功作出
      incentives and rewards to directors and employees of the Group who                       貢獻之本集團董事及僱員提供鼓勵及獎勵。計
      contribute to the success of the Group’s operations. The Scheme became                   劃於2012年6月22日生效，而除非被取消或經修
      effective on 22 June 2012 and, unless otherwise cancelled or amended,                    訂，否則該計劃將於當日起計十年內有效。有效
      shall be valid and effective for a period of 10 year from that date, after               期屆滿後，則不會再授出任何購股權，但在所有
      which period no further options will be issued but in all other respects the             其他方面，計劃條文仍具十足效力及作用。
      provisions of the Scheme shall remain in full force and effect.

      Typical payment terms which impact on the amount of contract liabilities of            對已確認物業開發合約負債金額構成影響之一
      property development recognised are as follows:                                        般支付條款如下：

      The Group receives certain amount of deposit from customers when they                  本集團於客戶簽署買賣協議時自彼等收取若干
      sign the sale and purchase agreements. These deposits are recognised as                訂金。該等訂金確認為合約負債，直至有關物業
      contract liabilities until the relevant properties are completed and ready for         落成並可供轉讓予客戶為止。
      hand-over to the customers.

      The Group may require the customers to pay off the remaining balance of                本集團可要求客戶於建造工程仍在進行之協定
      the consideration within an agreed timeframe while constructions are still             時間內而非於有關物業落成時償還剩餘代價。
      ongoing, rather than on the completion of the relevant properties. Such                有關提前付款計劃會導致於整個剩餘物業建造
      advance payment schemes result in contract liabilities being recognised                期間按全額合約價格確認合約負債。
      throughout the remaining property construction period for the full amount of
      the contract price.

 43 Summary of other potentially material accounting policies                         43   其他潛在重大會計政策概要
 43.1 Principles of consolidation and equity accounting                               43.1 合併及權益會計原則
      (a) Subsidiaries                                                                     (a) 附屬公司
           Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured entities) over which            附屬公司為本集團擁有控制權的所有實體
           the Group has control. The Group controls an entity where the Group                 （包 括 結 構 性 實 體）。當 本 集 團 因 參 與 一
           is exposed to, or has rights to, variable returns from its involvement              實體之業務而承擔或享有其可變回報，並
           with the entity and has the ability to affect those returns through                 有能力透過其指示該實體活動之權力影響
           its power to direct the activities of the entity. Subsidiaries are fully            有關回報時，即取得控制權。附屬公司自
           consolidated from the date on which control is transferred to the                   控制權轉移至本集團之日起全面合併。附
           Group. They are deconsolidated from the date that control ceases.                   屬公司自控制停止之日起解除合併。

            The acquisition method of accounting is used to account for business                 本集團使用收購會計法對業務合併進行會
            combinations by the Group (refer to note 43.2).                                      計處理（參閱附註43.2）。

            Inter-company transactions, balances and unrealised gains on                         公司之間之交易、集團公司間交易之結餘
            transactions between group companies are eliminated. Unrealised                      及未變現收益予以對銷。未變現虧損亦予
            losses are also eliminated unless the transaction provides evidence                  以對銷，除非交易有證據證明已轉讓資產
            of an impairment of the transferred asset. Accounting policies                       出現減值。附屬公司會計政策已按需要作
            of subsidiaries have been changed where necessary to ensure                          出變動，以確保與本集團所採納之政策貫
            consistency with the policies adopted by the Group.                                  徹一致。

            Non-controlling interests in the results and equity of subsidiaries                  附屬公司業績及權益中的非控股權益分別
            are shown separately in the consolidated statement of profit or loss,                於綜合損益表、全面收益表、權益變動表
            statement of comprehensive income, statement of changes in equity                    及財務狀況表中單獨呈列。
            and statement of financial position respectively.

      (b)   Associates                                                                     (b)   聯營公司
            Associates are all entities over which the Group has significant                     聯營公司指所有本集團對其擁有重大影響
            influence but not control or joint control. This is generally the case               力但無控制權或聯合控制權的實體。一般
            where the Group holds between 20% and 50% of the voting rights.                      情況下，本集團持有20%至50%投票權。於
            Investments in associates are accounted for using the equity method                  聯營公司之投資於初步按成本確認後採用
            of accounting, after initially being recognised at cost.                             權益會計法入賬。

43 Summary of other potentially material accounting policies                           43   其他潛在重大會計政策概要（續）
   (continued)
43.1 Principles of consolidation and equity accounting (continued)                     43.1 合併及權益會計原則（續）
     (c) Joint arrangements                                                                 (c) 合營安排
          Under HKFRS 11 Joint Arrangements investments in joint                                根據香港財務報告準則第11號聯合安排，
          arrangements are classified as either joint operations or joint                       於聯合安排的投資被分類為聯合經營或合
          ventures. The classification depends on the contractual rights and                    營公司。該分類取決於各投資者的合約性
          obligations of each investor, rather than the legal structure of the                  權利及責任，而非聯合安排的法律架構。
          joint arrangement. The Group has assessed the nature of its joint                     本集團已評估其聯合安排的性質，將其釐
          arrangements and determined them to be joint ventures.                                定為合營公司。

           Interests in joint ventures are accounted for using the equity method,                 於合營公司的投資於綜合財務狀況表於初
           after initially being recognised at cost in the consolidated statement                 步按成本確認後以權益法入賬。
           of financial position.

     (d)   Equity method                                                                    (d)   權益法
           Under the equity method of accounting, the investments are initially                   根據權益會計法，投資初始按成本確認，
           recognised at cost and adjusted thereafter to recognise the Group’s                    其後進行調整以於損益內確認本集團分佔
           share of the post-acquisition profits or losses of the investee in profit              被投資方的收購後溢利或虧損並於其他全
           or loss, and the Group’s share of movements in other comprehensive                     面收益確認其分佔被投資方其他全面收益
           income of the investee in other comprehensive income. Dividends                        的變動。已收或應收聯營公司及合營公司
           received or receivable from associates and joint ventures are                          的股息確認為投資賬面值扣減。
           recognised as a reduction in the carrying amount of the investment.

           Where the Group’s share of losses in an equity-accounted investment                    倘本集團分佔權益入賬投資的虧損等於或
           equals or exceeds its interest in the entity, including any other                      超過其於該實體的權益（包括任何其他無
           unsecured long-term receivables, the Group does not recognize                          抵 押 長 期 應 收 款 項），則 本 集 團 不 會 確 認
           further losses, unless it has incurred obligations or made payments on                 進一步虧損，除非已代表另一實體承擔責
           behalf of the other entity.                                                            任或作出付款。

           Unrealised gains on transactions between the Group and its                             本集團與其聯營公司及合營公司之間交易
           associates and joint ventures are eliminated to the extent of                          的未變現收益按本集團於該等實體的權益
           the Group’s interest in these entities. Unrealised losses are also                     予以對銷。未變現虧損亦予以對銷，但如
           eliminated unless the transaction provides evidence of an impairment                   有關交易提供所轉讓資產減值之證據則除
           of the asset transferred. Accounting policies of equity-accounted                      外。權益入賬被投資方的會計政策已在需
           investees have been changed where necessary to ensure consistency                      要時作出調整，以確保與本集團所採納會
           with the policies adopted by the Group.                                                計政策一致。

           The carrying amount of equity-accounted investments is tested for                      權益入賬投資賬面值根據附註43.9所述政
           impairment in accordance with the policy described in note 43.9.                       策進行減值測試。

      (e) Changes in ownership interests                                                    (e) 擁有權權益變動

           the Group. A change in ownership interest results in an adjustment                   有權權益變動導致控股與非控股權益賬面
           between the carrying amounts of the controlling and non-controlling                  值的調整，以反映其於附屬公司的相關權

           interests and any consideration paid or received is recognised in a                  佔權益中之單獨儲備項目內確認。

            This fair value becomes the initial carrying amount for the purposes              確認。就其後入賬列作聯營公司、合營公
            of subsequently accounting for the retained interest as an associate,             司或金融資產的保留權益而言，該公允值
            joint venture or financial asset. In addition, any amounts previously             成為初始賬面值。此外，之前在其他全面
            recognised in other comprehensive income in respect of that entity                收益中就該實體確認的任何金額猶如本集
            are accounted for as if the Group had directly disposed of the                    團已直接出售相關資產和負債般入賬。這
            related assets or liabilities. This may mean that amounts previously              可能代表先前在其他全面收益內確認之金

43.2 Business combinations                                                               43.2 業務合併
     The acquisition method of accounting is used to account for all business                 本集團採用收購會計法就所有業務合併入賬，
     combinations, regardless of whether equity instruments or other assets are               而無論有否收購股權工具或其他資產。收購一
     acquired. The consideration transferred for the acquisition of a subsidiary              間附屬公司的轉讓代價包括：
     comprises the:

     •     fair values of the assets transferred                                              •   為所轉讓資產公允值

     •     liabilities incurred to the former owners of the acquired business                 •   對被收購業務前擁有人所產生的負債

     •     equity interests issued by the group                                               •   本集團所發行股權

     •     fair value of any asset or liability resulting from a contingent                   •   或有代價安排產生的任何資產或負債的公
           consideration arrangement, and                                                         允值，及

     •     fair value of any pre-existing equity interest in the subsidiary.                  •   附屬公司任何先前存在股權的公允值。

     Identifiable assets acquired and liabilities and contingent liabilities assumed          於業務合併時所收購的可識別資產及所承擔的
     in a business combination are, with limited exceptions, measured initially               負債及或有負債，初始按收購當日的公允值計
     at their fair values at the acquisition date. The Group recognises any non-              量，惟少數例外情況除外。本集團根據個別收購
     controlling interest in the acquired entity on an acquisition-by-acquisition             基準按公允值或非控股權益應佔被收購實體可
     basis either at fair value or at the non-controlling interest’s proportionate            識別資產淨值的比例確認於被收購實體的任何
     share of the acquired entity’s net identifiable assets.                                  非控股權益。

     Concentration test as prescribed by HKFRS 3 “Business Combination”                       本集團已應用香港財務報告準則第3號「業務合
     has been applied by the Group to consider whether the set of activities                  併」所規定的集中度測試，以考慮所收購附屬公
     and assets of subsidiaries acquired are individually businesses. If the fair             司的一系列活動及資產是否為個別業務。倘所
     value of total net assets acquired substantially equals to the fair value of             收購資產淨值總額的公允值與已收購可識別資
     an identifiable asset acquired, the transaction would be accounted for as                產的公允值大致相若，則交易將作為資產收購
     asset acquisition instead of business acquisition. The consideration is then             而非業務收購入賬。屆時會將代價直接分配至
     directly allocated to the individual identifiable assets acquired and liabilities        收購的個別可識別資產及承擔的負債。
     assumed.

     Acquisition-related costs are expensed as incurred.                                      收購相關成本於產生時支銷。

    (continued)
 43.2 Business combinations (continued)                                                   43.2 業務合併（續）
      The excess of the:                                                                       下列各項：

      •     consideration transferred,                                                         •   所轉讓代價，

      •     amount of any non-controlling interest in the acquired entity, and                 •   被收購實體的任何非控股權益金額，及

      •     acquisition-date fair value of any previous equity interest in the                 •   於被收購實體先前的任何股權於收購日期
            acquired entity                                                                        的公允值，

      over the fair value of the net identifiable assets acquired is recorded as               超出所收購可識別資產淨值的公允值時，其差
      goodwill. If those amounts are less than the fair value of the net identifiable          額以商譽列賬。倘上述金額低於所收購業務可
      assets of the business acquired, the difference is recognised directly in                識別資產淨值的公允值，有關差額會作為一項
      profit or loss as a bargain purchase.                                                    議價購買直接於損益確認。

      Where settlement of any part of cash consideration is deferred, the                      倘任何部分現金代價的結算獲遞延，日後應付
      amounts payable in the future are discounted to their present value as at                金額貼現至彼等於兌換日期的現值。所使用的
      the date of exchange. The discount rate used is the entity’s incremental                 貼現率是實體的增量借貸率，即在可比條款和
      borrowing rate, being the rate at which a similar borrowing could be                     條件下，可以從獨立融資人處獲得類似借貸的
      obtained from an independent financier under comparable terms and                        利率。或然代價分類為權益或金融負債。分類為
      conditions. Contingent consideration is classified either as equity or a                 金融負債的金額其後重新計量至公允值，公允
      financial liability. Amounts classified as a financial liability are subsequently        值變動於損益確認。
      remeasured to fair value with changes in fair value recognised in profit or
      loss.

      If the business combination is achieved in stages, the acquisition date                  倘業務合併分階段進行，則收購方先前持有被
      carrying value of the acquirer’s previously held equity interest in the                  收購方的股權於收購日期的賬面值於收購日期
      acquiree is remeasured to fair value at the acquisition date. Any gains or               重新按公允值計量。因有關重新計量所產生的
      losses arising from such remeasurement are recognised in profit or loss.                 任何收益或虧損乃於損益中確認。

43.2 Business combinations (continued)                                               43.2 業務合併（續）
     Disposals of subsidiaries                                                            出售附屬公司
     When the Group ceases to have control, any retained interest in the entity           倘本集團不再擁有控制權，於該實體之任何保
     is re-measured to its fair value at the date when control is lost, with the          留權益在失去控制權當日按其公允值重新計
     change in carrying amount recognised in profit or loss. The fair value is            量，而賬面值變動則於損益內確認。就其後入賬
     the initial carrying amount for the purposes of subsequently accounting              列作聯營公司、合營公司或金融資產的保留權
     for the retained interest as an associate, joint venture or financial asset.         益而言，該公允值為初始賬面值。此外，之前在
     In addition, any amounts previously recognised in other comprehensive                其他全面收益中就該實體確認的任何金額猶如
     income in respect of that entity are accounted for as if the Group had               本集團已直接出售相關資產和負債般入賬。這
     directly disposed of the related assets or liabilities. It means the amounts         可能代表先前在其他全面收益內確認之金額重
     previously recognised in other comprehensive income are reclassified to              新分類至損益或轉撥至適用香港財務報告準則

43.3 Separate financial statements                                                   43.3 獨立財務報表
     Investments in subsidiaries are accounted for at cost less impairment.               附屬公司投資按成本扣除減值列賬。成本亦包
     Cost also includes direct attributable costs of investment. The results of           括投資的直接應佔成本。附屬公司的業績由本
     subsidiaries are accounted for by the Company on the basis of dividend               公司已收及應收的股息入賬。
     received and receivable.

     Impairment testing of the investments in subsidiaries is required upon               倘於附屬公司之投資產生之股息超過附屬公司
     receiving dividends from these investments if the dividend exceeds the               於宣派股息期間之全面收益總額或倘該投資於
     total comprehensive income of the subsidiary in the period the dividend              單獨財務報表內之賬面值超過被投資方資產
     is declared or if the carrying amount of the investment in the separate              淨額（包括商譽）於綜合財務報表內之賬面值，
     financial statements exceeds the carrying amount in the consolidated                 則於收到該等股息時須對該等投資進行減值測
     financial statements of the investee’s net assets including goodwill.                試。

43.4 Segment reporting                                                               43.4 分類報告
     Operating segments are reported in a manner consistent with the internal             經營分類按照向首席經營決策者提供的內部報
     reporting provided to the chief operating decision maker.                            告貫徹一致的方式報告。

     The Group’s most senior executive management assesses the financial                  本集團最高行政管理人員評估本集團的財務表
     performance and position of the Group and makes strategic decisions of               現及狀況，並就向本集團不同業務線及地區分
     allocating resources to, and assessing the performance of, the Group’s               配資源及評估表現作出戰略決策。有關最高行
     various lines of business and geographical locations. They have been                 政管理人員已確定為主要經營決策者，由行政
     identified as being the chief operating decision maker, consists of the chief        總裁、財務總監及企業規劃經理組成。
     executive officer, the chief financial officer and the managers for corporate
     planning.

      (a) Functional and presentation currency                                              (a) 功能和列報貨幣

            liabilities denominated in foreign currencies at year end exchange                    與合資格現金流量對沖及合資格投資淨額

 43.6 Property, plant and equipment                                                      43.6 物業、廠房及設備
      Property, plant and equipment are stated at historical cost less depreciation           物業、廠房及設備均以歷史成本扣除折舊及任
      and any impairment losses. Historical cost includes expenditure that is                 何減值虧損列賬。歷史成本包括購買該等項目
      directly attributable to the acquisition of the items.                                  直接應佔的開支。

      Subsequent costs are included in the asset’s carrying amount or recognised              後續成本只有在很可能為本集團帶來與該項目
      as a separate asset, as appropriate, only when it is probable that future               有關的未來經濟利益，而該項目的成本能可靠
      economic benefits associated with the item will flow to the Group and                   計量時，才包括在資產的賬面值或確認為一項
      the cost of the item can be measured reliably. The carrying amount of                   單獨資產（如適用）。已更換部分的賬面值已被
      the replaced part is derecognised. All other repairs and maintenance are                終止確認。所有其他維修及保養開支乃於其產
      charged to profit or loss during the financial period in which they are                 生之財政期間內自損益扣除。
      incurred.

            the goodwill is allocated represents the lowest level within the entity                 組別為實體內就內部管理目的而監察商譽
            at which the goodwill is monitored for internal management purposes.                    之最低層次。商譽乃於經營分類層次進行
            Goodwill is monitored at the operating segment level.                                   監察。

            Goodwill is not amortized but its impairment reviews are undertaken                     商譽不予攤銷，但每年進行減值檢討，或
            annually or more frequently if events or changes in circumstances                       當有事件出現或情況改變顯示可能出現減
            indicate a potential impairment. The carrying value of the CGU                          值時，作出更頻密檢討。包含商譽之現金
            containing the goodwill is compared to the recoverable amount, which                    產生單位之賬面值與可收回金額作比較，
            is the higher of value in use and the fair value less costs of disposal.                可收回金額為使用價值與公允值減出售成
            Any impairment is recognised immediately as an expense and is not                       本之較高者。任何減值即時確認為開支，
            subsequently reversed.                                                                  且其後不會撥回。

      (b)   Concession right                                                                  (b)   特許經營權

            the management rights, the Group shall hand over the relevant project

 43.9 Impairment of financial assets                                                     43.9 金融資產減值
      The Group recognises a loss allowance for expected credit losses (“ECL”) on             本集團就根據香港財務報告準則第9號須予減值
      financial assets which are subject to impairment under HKFRS 9 (including               的金融資產（包括貿易及其他應收款、給予合營
      trade and other receivables, loans to joint ventures and associates, cash               公司及聯營公司之貸款、現金及現金等價物、受
      and cash equivalents, restricted cash and bank deposits and financial                   限 制 現 金 及 銀 行 存 款 以 及 財 務 擔 保）的 預 期 信
      guarantee).                                                                             用損失（「預期信用損失」）確認損失撥備。

       The amount of ECL is updated at each reporting date to reflect changes in              預期信用損失金額於各報告日更新，反映自初
       credit risk since initial recognition. Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that            始確認起的信用風險變動。存續期預期信用損
       will result from all possible default events over the expected life of the             失指相關工具的預期使用期內所有可能的違約
       relevant instrument. In contrast, 12-month ECL (“12m ECL”) represents the              事件產生之預期信用損失。相反，12個月預期信
       portion of lifetime ECL that is expected to result from default events that            用損失（「 12個月預期信用損失」）指於報告日期
       are possible within 12 months after the reporting date. Assessment are                 後12個月內可能發生的違約事件預期導致之存
       done based on the Group’s historical credit loss experience, adjusted for              續期預期信用損失部分。評估乃根據本集團的
       factors that are specific to the debtors, general economic conditions and an           歷史信用損失經驗進行，並根據債務人特有的
       assessment of both the current conditions at the reporting date as well as             因素、一般經濟狀況以及對報告日期當前狀況
       the forecast of future conditions.                                                     的評估以及對未來狀況的預測作出調整。

43.10 Offsetting financial instruments                                                   43.10 抵銷金融工具

      simultaneously. The legally enforceable right must not be contingent on                  須不得依賴未來事件而定，而在一般業務過程

 43.13 Contract assets, contract liabilities and contract cost                         43.13 合約資產、合約負債及合約成本
       Upon entering into a contract with a buyer, the Group obtains rights to               在與買家簽訂合約時，本集團有權獲得來自買
       receive consideration from the buyer and assumes performance obligations              家支付的合約價款，與此同時承擔向買家轉移
       to transfer goods or provide services to the buyer. The combination of                貨物或提供服務的履約義務。這些權利和履行
       those rights and performance obligations gives rise to a net asset or a               義務的結合導致了淨資產或淨負債的產生，這
       net liability depending on the relationship between the remaining rights              取決於剩餘權利和履行義務之間的關係。合約
       and the performance obligations. The contract is an asset and recognised              計量的剩餘收款權超過了剩餘履約義務的部分
       as contract assets if the measure of the remaining rights exceeds the                 確認為合約資產。反之，剩餘履約義務超過剩餘
       measure of the remaining performance obligations. Conversely, the contract            收款權的部分確認為合約負債。
       is a liability and recognised as contract liabilities if the measure of the
       remaining performance obligations exceeds the measure of the remaining
       rights.

      Other than the costs which are capitalised as inventories, property, plant            除資本化為存貨、物業、廠房及設備以及無形資
      and equipment and intangible assets, costs incurred to fulfil a contract with         產的成本外，為履行與客戶的合約而產生的成
      a customer are capitalised as an asset if all of the following criteria are           本，若符合下列所有條件，則資本化為資產：
      met:

      (a)   The costs relate directly to a contract or to an anticipated contract           (a)   該成本直接與實體可明確識別的合約或預
            that the entity can specifically identify.                                            期合約有關。

      (b)   The costs generate or enhance resources of the entity that will be              (b)   該成本產生或增強實體未來用於履行（或
            used in satisfying (or in continuing to satisfy) performance obligations              持續履行）履約責任的資源。
            in the future.

      (c) The costs are expected to be recovered.                                           (c)   該成本預期可以收回。

      The capitalised contract costs are amortised and charged to the statement             資本化合約成本按系統化基準攤銷，並在損益
      of profit or loss on a systematic basis that is consistent with the transfer          表中扣除，該基準與向客戶轉讓資產相關的貨
      to the customer of the goods or services to which the asset relates. Other            品或服務的基準一致。其他合約成本於產生時
      contract costs are expensed as incurred.                                              支銷。

43.15 Share capital                                                                     43.15 股本
     Ordinary shares are classified as equity.                                               普通股會被分類為權益。

      Where any Group company purchases the Company’s equity share capital                   倘 任 何 集 團 公 司 購 買 本 公 司 的 權 益 股 本（庫 存
      (treasury shares), the consideration paid, including any directly attributable         股份），已支付的代價（包括任何直接所佔的增
      incremental costs (net of income taxes), is deducted from equity                       量成本（扣除所得稅）從本公司權益股東應佔的
      attributable to the owners of Company until the shares are cancelled or                權益中扣除，直至股份被註銷或重新發行為止。
      reissued. Where such shares are subsequently reissued, any consideration               倘有關股份其後重新發行，則所收取的任何代價
      received, net of any directly attributable incremental transaction costs and           （扣除任何直接應佔的增量交易成本及有關所
      the related income tax effect is included in equity attributable to the owners         得稅影響）計入本公司權益股東應佔的權益。
      of Company.

43.16 Government grants                                                                 43.16 政府補助
      Grants from the government are recognised at their fair value where there               倘可合理地保證將會收到政府補助及本集團將
      is a reasonable assurance that the grant will be received and the Group will            符合所有附帶條件，則補助按其公允值確認。
      comply with all attached conditions.

      Government grants relating to costs are deferred and recognised in the                 與成本有關之政府補助遞延處理，並於利用該
      profit or loss over the period necessary to match them with the costs that             等補助用以補償該等成本的期間在損益內確
      they are intended to compensate.                                                       認。

      Government grants relating to the purchase of property, plant and                      有關購買物業、廠房及設備的政府補助則在非
      equipment are included in non-current liabilities as deferred income and               流動負債中列作遞延收入，並在損益內以直線
      are credited to profit or loss on a straight-line basis over the expected lives        法於相關資產預計年期攤銷。
      of the related assets.

43.17 Trade and other payables                                                          43.17 貿易及其他應付款
      Trade payables are obligations to pay for goods or services that have been              應付賬款為於日常業務過程中從供應商購入貨
      acquired in the ordinary course of business from suppliers. Trade payables              品或服務之付款責任。如果付款於一年或以內
      are classified as current liabilities if payment is due within one year or less        （或倘時間較長，則於正常之業務運營週期內）
      (or in the normal operating cycle of the business if longer). If not, they are          到期，則將應付賬款分類為流動負債。否則，呈
      presented as non-current liabilities.                                                   列為非流動負債。

      Trade and other payables are recognised initially at fair value and                    貿易及其他應付款初始按公允值確認，其後以
      subsequently measured at amortised cost using the effective interest                   實際利率法按攤銷成本計量。
      method.

 43.18 Borrowings                                                                         43.18 借貸
       Borrowings are recognised initially at fair value, net of transaction costs              借貸初始按公允值扣除所產生的交易成本確
       incurred. Borrowings are subsequently measured at amortised cost;                        認。借貸其後按攤銷成本計量，如扣除交易成本
       any difference between the proceeds (net of transaction costs) and the                   之後的所得收益與贖回價值出現差額，則於借
       redemption value is recognised in profit or loss over the period of the                  貸期內以實際利率法在損益內確認。
       borrowings using the effective interest method.

      Borrowings are removed from the consolidated statement financial position                當合約中規定之責任解除、取消或屆滿時，借貸
      when the obligation specified in the contract is discharged, cancelled                   自綜合財務狀況表中剔除。已消除或轉讓予另
      or expired. The difference between the carrying amount of a financial                    一 方 之 金 融 負 債 之 賬 面 值 與 已 支 付 代 價（包 括
      liability that has been extinguished or transferred to another party and the             任 何 已 轉 讓 之 非 現 金 資 產 或 所 承 擔 之 負 債）之
      consideration paid, including any non-cash assets transferred or liabilities             間之差額，在損益中確認為財務成本。
      assumed, is recognised in profit or loss as finance costs.

      Where the terms of a financial liability are renegotiated and the entity                 倘重新議定一項金融負債的條款，而實體向債
      issues equity instruments to a creditor to extinguish all or part of the                 權 人 發 行 權 益 工 具 以 消 除 所 有 或 部 分 負 債（債
      liability (debt for equity swap), a gain or loss is recognised in profit or loss,        務或權益互換），則相關盈虧於損益內確認，並
      which is measured as the difference between the carrying amount of the                   按該金融負債的賬面值與發行的權益工具的公
      financial liability and the fair value of the equity instruments issued.                 允值之差額計量。

      Borrowings are classified as current liabilities unless the Group has an                 除非本集團有權無條件將債務清償遞延至報告
      unconditional right to defer settlement of the liability for at least 12 months          期 後 至 少12個 月，否 則 借 貸 將 被 分 類 為 流 動 負
      after the reporting period.                                                              債。

 43.19 Borrowing costs                                                                    43.19 借貸成本
       General and specific borrowing costs directly attributable to the acquisition,           直 接 歸 屬 於 收 購、興 建 或 生 產 合 資 格 資 產（須
       construction or production of qualifying assets, which are assets that                   經 較 長 籌 備 期 方 可 作 擬 定 用 途 或 銷 售 的 資 產）
       necessarily take a substantial period of time to get ready for their intended            的一般及特定借貸成本會加入該等資產的成本
       use or sale, are added to the cost of those assets, until such time as the               中，直至資產大致上備妥可作擬定用途或銷售
       assets are substantially ready for their intended use or sale.                           為止。

      Investment income earned on the temporary investment of specific                         在特定借款撥作合資格資產的支出前暫時用作
      borrowings pending their expenditure on qualifying assets is deducted from               投資所賺取的投資收入會自可撥充資本的借貸
      the borrowing costs eligible for capitalisation.                                         成本中扣除。

      All other borrowing costs are recognised in profit or loss in the period in              所有其他借貸成本於其產生期間在損益內確
      which they are incurred.                                                                 認。
